2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 30 年期公債殖利率上週二升至 5.34%的 19 年以來高點，然而美國財政部
周三意外宣布長債回購規模翻倍，此舉造成美債殖利率大幅回落，指標 10 年期
美債殖利率下跌 6 bps 至 4.647%，30 年期美債殖利率一度重挫 9bps 至 5.19%，
同時帶動美元指數走跌 ，惟聯準會七月會議記錄顯示 ， 委員們加深對於美國通膨
上升的擔憂 ，部分官員傾向升息 ， 會議紀要偏向鷹派令美元指數收復部分跌幅。
⚫ 上週澳洲公布七月就業數據，失業率 4.5%，升至近 5 年高位，就業人數意外下
降 ， 顯示勞動力市場正在降溫 ， 緩解澳洲央行再次升息的壓力 ， 數據公布後澳幣
一度下跌 ， 惟澳洲央行副總裁表示若通膨繼續上行仍有再次升息空間， 偏鷹立場
令澳幣獲得支撐，AUD/USD 升至近兩個月高點。
⚫ 日本第二季經濟成長明顯放緩 ， 家庭消費與企業投資疲弱 ， 抵消了出口的強勁表

現。GDP 年增率僅增長 1.1%，低於市場預期的 2.0%；季增為 0.3%，也不及預
估的 0.5%。惟 7 月核心通膨率升至 1.8%，反映企業將日幣貶值及伊朗戰爭推高
的進口成本轉嫁給消費者 ， 這為日本央行進一步加息提供了更多理由 ， 給予日幣
部分支撐。
⚫ 中國 7 月工業、投資與消費全面低於市場預期，失業率也升至三個月高點，顯
示中國面臨內需走軟的壓力。另一方面，中國央行在 8 月維持一年期與五年期
以上 LPR 分別在 3.00%與 3.50%， 已連續 15 個月保持不變 。惟中國出口仍維持
強勁，貿易順差令人民緩步走升。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Aug/24/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 31.774，低點在 32.020，以 31.848 作收，較前一週收盤價
升值 1 角 9.8 分。 
上週台幣震盪走升，週初，因近期美國通膨數據顯示並未惡化，淡化市場對於 9 月
聯準會升息預期，美元指數下跌，台幣隨非美幣別反彈，升破32 整數關口。週間，
美國財政部宣布提高長天期國債回購規模至少一倍，令長天期美債殖利率下跌，帶
動美元指數跌破 99 整數關口至 98.77，台幣走升至 31.774 週內高點；臨近週末，外
資及投信連續買超台股並匯入，台幣偏升震盪於32.000 整數關口下方，終場在央行
調節之下收在 31.848。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏升。就國際市場而言，美國財長貝森特預告將公布更多的財政
整頓細節，預估將限制長端殖利率，進而減弱市場對於美元資產的信心，將限制美
元指數上漲空間；就台灣市場來看，台灣 7 月外銷訂單金額達 979.4 億美元，創歷
年單月新高，年增率由 59.4 上升至 61.9%，基本面強勁將持續支撐台幣匯價，加以
外資及投信偏向買超台股並匯入，台股持續於高檔震盪，預估將有利台幣表現，惟
匯價逢低仍吸引不少進口商買美元 ， 預估將限制台幣升值的速度跟幅度 。 綜合以上 ，
預估本週台幣走勢偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Aug/24/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣高檔震盪。週初，美伊談判缺乏進展，推動油價明顯走高，美元指數由
99.3 小幅反彈至 99.7，人民幣由 6.7400 整數關口小幅回落至 6.7480；週間，美國財
政部宣布提高長天期國債回購規模至少一倍，令長天期美債殖利率下跌，帶動美元
指數跌破 99 整數關口至 98.77，人民幣升破 6.7200 整數關口；臨近週末，美國公布
8 月製造業 PMI 由上個月的 53.9 下滑至 53.2，美元指數持續承壓於 99 整數關口下
方，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7193，週線升值 250 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.7215，週
線升值 207 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升 。 國際市場方面 ， 美元指數隨美債殖利率反彈而小幅回升 ，
反映利差優勢對美元仍有支撐將限制人民幣走升的幅度。不過美國財長貝森特預告
將公布更多的財政整頓細節，預估將限制長端殖利率，進而減弱市場對於美元資產
的信心，將限制美元指數上漲空間，人民幣有望延續升值走勢。中國國內方面，人
行中間價格繼續創高，釋放人民幣偏強信號，支撐人民幣延續升值的方向。技術面
方面，USD/CNH 匯價居於各天期均線下方，且均線向下發散，空方動能較盛，顯示
人民幣空方格局不變。綜合以上，預估本週人民幣走勢偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.7000-6.7500

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Aug/24/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 小幅下跌。週初USD/JPY 大致在 158.8 至 159.8 區間波動；週三
晚間美國財政部宣布提高回購長債規模消息，USD/JPY 一度走低至 158 整數價位，
後因買盤進場以及美元指數回升，USD/JPY 上行至 159 整數關卡盤整；臨近週末，
USD/JPY 續於 158.3 至 159.1 區間波動，終場收在 158.93，週線下跌 37 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈日本7 月進出口數
據均創下新高，出口年增 23.2%高於預期的 19.9%，主因為 AI 相關數據中心強勁需
求和日圓貶值推動，而能源成本上升則使進口年增 27.8%高於預期的 26.5%，貿易
收支連續三個月陷入逆差；此外 7 月核心 CPI 由 6 月 1.6%升至 1.8%顯示通膨有所
增速將支持日本央行於 9 月升息，有利日幣走勢。二、美國8 月製造業 PMI 由 7 月
53.9 降至 53.2 為五個月來新低，主因為供應鏈受阻；然而服務業強勁擴張，服務業
PMI 由 7 月的 54.6 上升至 56.8，帶動綜合 PMI 由 7 月的 54.5 上升至 56.0 創四年來
新高 ， 顯示美國經濟依舊保持韌性 ， 將為美元帶來支撐 。 三 、 就技術面而言 ，USD/JPY
匯價一度下行至年線 158.3 後反彈，後持續在年線 158.3 至月線 159.4 區間內盤整，
在有效突破前暫以區間震盪看待 。綜合以上 ，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可
能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：158.50-160.20 
前週交易區間 
158.00 159.77

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
         Aug/24/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初 EUR/USD 突破 1.16 整數關卡，但美伊未延長
停火協議帶動避險需求回落 1.16 下方整理 ； 週間受到避險情緒升溫影響 ，EUR/USD
在 1.1565 到 1.1590 之間窄幅震盪，週三晚間美國財政部宣布提高回購長債規模，
EUR/USD 走升最高來至 1.1710 後回落至 1.1670 附近；臨近週末美國擴大回購長債
與美國 7 月 PMI 數據使得 EUR/USD 震盪整理 ， 終場收在1.1679， 週線上漲110 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏升，主要原因如下：一、近期通膨數據顯示歐洲依
然面臨通膨壓力，其中，歐元區 7 月 CPI 及核心 CPI 分別為 2.9%及 2.5%，符合市
場預期但高於 6 月數值；而德國 7 月 PPI 月增 1.1%、年增 3%為三年來最大漲幅，
皆高於市場預期且相較 6 月的月減 0.3%、年增 1.8%有顯著增速；此外，歐洲央行
首席經濟學家連恩表示當前 3%通膨仍高於歐洲央行目標，不能低估偏離目標的幅
度，通膨維持高位且歐洲央行偏鷹派態度，有利歐元上行。二、受惠於新訂單增加
和出口恢復成長，歐元區 8 月綜合 PMI 由 7 月 52.0 升至 52.1 優於市場預期，且為
去年 11 月以來最高，歐元區經濟持穩將為歐元帶來支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線
圖中，EUR/USD 強勢站上年線 1.1630，壓力轉為支撐位，且匯價居於各天期均線之
上 ， 短線多方動能尚未消退 ， 然而1.17 整數關卡上方有賣壓待消化 ， 將限縮EUR/USD
上行幅度。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏升的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1600-1.1800 
前週交易區間 
1.1558 1.1711

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                         新台幣利率走勢分析

115.08.17-115.08.21 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15106 1.864%~1.910% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.840%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週外資連續匯入 ， 令外商銀行短錢供給充裕 ， 資金情勢
穩中偏鬆，惟銀行間吸收資金成本仍高 ， 對短票利率有所堅持 ， 令短率持穩於
近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本周部份買盤進場承接 10 年指標券 A15106，帶動該券殖利率回落，等殖系
統成交區間在 1.864%~1.91%，5 年期指標券 A15108 則未成交，最近成交利
率為 1.84%。 
儘管有利率逢高買盤支撐，緩解台債偏空氛圍，惟美伊僵局下油價仍緩步墊
高 ， 通膨升溫潛在風險仍存 ， 目前國際債市利率依舊維持高位 ， 加上後續台灣
央行貨幣政策仍有緊縮壓力 ，料台債殖利率短線仍將呈高檔盤整格局 ，預估5
年券交易區間在 1.78%~1.83%、10 年券交易區間在 1.85%~1.90%。

營收走勢圖

電機機械  
上銀(2049.TT) 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，精密線性傳動元件交期持續拉長

AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產
的精密線性傳動元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備 ， 在半導體產業CapEx
確立的情況下，預期這波拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年
的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度 EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出
現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給予 35x 本益比，目
標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於 AI 與半導體設備當中，這
波景氣上升循環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線
性滑軌的交期從去年年底 3 個月，拉長到現在的 4~5 個月；滾珠螺桿
交期從去年年底 4 個月，拉長到現在 5~6 個月。從營收來看，扣除 2
月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收 YoY 從 3 月的
17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收不但在
成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從 6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，
需求非常強勁，已開始規劃明年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好
這波由 AI 與半導體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年，預估上銀 2026/27
年營收為 300.1/378.2 億元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求 ， 公司於今年3 月宣布調漲 7~15%， 已於6 月開始反應 。
2Q26 毛利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所
致，預估 3Q26 漲價效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研
究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛利率為 34.3%/35.6%， 分別年增5.5/1.3
個百分點，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。 
這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看好在交期不斷拉長
的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。(上一波 2021-2022 年上升
循環中毛利率最高來到 37-38%)

一、 一、公司介紹 
   上銀科技成立於 1989 年 ， 專注於線性滑軌 、 滾珠螺桿與單軸機器人等精密線性
傳動元件的製造 ， 為該領域的全球領導廠商之一 。 除了持續研發線性傳動產品以外 ，
也自行開發出多種半導體自動化設備 ，產品廣泛應用於汽車 、 半導體 、 醫療 、 國防 、
航太及食品等產業。上銀憑藉自有品牌 「HIWIN」 的深厚研發實力與品質行銷全世
界，目前為全球第二大生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿的廠商，第一大為日商 THK 
(6481.T)。1H26 產品營收占比﹔線性滑軌 60%、 滾珠螺桿20%、 工業機器人12%、
其他 8%。2025 年地區營收占比﹔台灣 44%、中國 21%、德國 16%、美國 5%、
日本 4%、其他 10%。(地區營收 2025 年已是最新公布)

二、 產業分析 
上銀主要產品為線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿，產品功能如下﹔ 
1. 線性滑軌﹔一種線性傳動元件，由滑塊與滑軌組成，透過內部的滾珠或滾  
柱循環系統來降低摩擦，讓平台保持平行穩定的直線運動。  
2. 滾珠螺桿﹔一種線性傳動元件，由螺桿、螺帽與鋼珠組成，利用鋼珠在螺桿與
螺帽間循環滾動，將旋轉運動轉換為直線運動。

圖四、滾珠螺桿

商業模式 
線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿為工具機零組件 ， 工具機產業商業模式分為以下三種 (以 AOI
檢測設備為例)﹔ 
1. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備零組件廠用五軸加工機生產高精度定位平台  ➨ 設備商用高精度定位
平台與其他零組件組裝成 AOI 檢測設備。  
2. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備商用五軸加工機自行生產所需的高精度定位平台或其他零組件。 
3. 工具機零組件廠生產的線性滑軌同時是工具機零組件與設備零組件  ➨ 設備
商直接購買線性滑軌來組裝。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業趨勢 
JMTBA (日本工具機協會) 每月會將日本所有工具機 OEM 廠的訂單加總 ， 公布 「日
本工具機訂單金額」 ，其產品包含多軸加工機、CNC 車床、滾齒機……等等，由於
日本擁有全球規模前幾大的工具機 OEM 廠，如 DMG、Yamazaki、Okuma，且多
年來產品遍布各個產業所累積的經驗也使得這些公司不論在自研控制器的演算
法，還是機器長時間運轉下維持的精度與溫度都與其他國家有技術上的差距。  
因此，在各行各業景氣轉好，需要大量的工具機時，JMTBA 公布的訂單金額被視
為是工具機產業最重要的觀察指標，工具機 OEM 廠訂單增加 ➨ 增加向工具機
零組件廠的拉貨。 
觀察過去十年日本工具機訂單的走勢，可發現景氣大約呈現兩年上升、兩年下降
的循環。上一次的上升週期為 2021-22 年疫情加上塞港，全球各產業瘋狂拉貨，
使得工具機訂單 YoY 從 2020/11 的-6%，最高拉升到 2021/5 的 142%，一直到
2022/10 訂單 YoY 才轉負 。 塞港造成的恐慌式補庫存為工具機產業帶來20 年來不
曾有過的成長率 ， 也使得隨後各產業去化庫存時 ， 工具機這次的景氣休息得較久 ，
直到 2H25 才再次迎來上升週期。

圖六、日本工具機訂單及 YoY

資料來源：JMTBA

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖七、上銀過去十年股價走勢圖

資料來源：Wind 
（註：與日本工具機訂單 YoY 高度聯動）

而在此波的上升週期，生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿等工具機零組件全球市占前二的
廠商，THK、上銀一致提到這一次的需求來自 AI 與半導體，且需求非常強勁。 
細看 AI 與半導體產業，其中涵蓋了相當多工具機的蹤跡，舉凡伺服器散熱所需的
水冷板、快接頭及熱交換器，到伺服器機殼、Compute/GPU Tray 與各種結構件，
都需要以工具機進行切削或沖壓。半導體產業中，在製程升級與產能擴充的過程
需要大量的半導體設備 ，不論在前 、 中 、 後段的設備都須使用線性傳動元件 ， 如蝕
刻機、AOI 檢測設備、各類型 Bonder 等等。 
產品規格方面，AI 與半導體所需的工具機精度更高，使得 THK、上銀這種技術領
先的廠商進一步受惠 。 一般工業用工具機僅需使用H/N 級的線性滑軌與 C5/C7 級
的滾珠螺桿，生產水冷板與快接頭時需使用 SP/P 級的線性滑軌與 C3 級的滾珠螺
桿；在Die Bonder (晶粒鍵合機)與 Flip Chip Bonder (倒裝鍵合機)上則需使用 UP/SP
級的線性滑軌與 C0/C1 級的滾珠螺桿，生產高精度的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿毛利率
顯著高於精度較低的。

SP ≈ 7 μm

出貨給半導體製程廠商。

在 AI 的快速發展下，半導體製程升級與產能擴張帶動半導體資本支出持續增加，

小結 
歷經 2022 年後的全球各產業庫存去化，工具機景氣委靡了接近三年的期間，如
今挾帶著 AI 伺服器與半導體設備需求強勁成長，再次迎來新一波景氣上升循環。
從水冷板、快接頭到各類半導體設備，皆帶動工具機需求快速升溫， 相關廠商也
同步指出需求強勁、訂單持續增加，交期明顯拉長。 
更重要的是，這波需求主要來自半導體設備等高精度應用領域，所需線性滑軌與
滾珠螺桿規格高於一般工業產品，上銀除受惠於工具機景氣重新向上外，亦可進
一步受惠於高規格精密線性傳動元件需求成長。

### Page 9

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 9

三、 三、營運狀況 
1. 1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於半導體設備當中，這波景氣上升循
環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線性滑軌的交期從去年
年底 3 個月 ，到五月法說會提到 3.5 個月 ， 再到八月份交期已經拉長到 5 個月 ；
滾珠螺桿交期從去年年底 4 個月，到五月法說會的 4.5 個月，再到現在的 6 個
月。從營收來看，扣除 2 月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收
YoY 從 3 月的 17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收
不但在成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，需
求非常強勁，已開始規劃明 年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好這波半導
體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年 ，預估上銀 2026/27 年營收為 300.1/378.2 億
元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

圖十二、上銀各產品交期

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求，公司於今年 3 月宣布調漲 7~15%，已於 6 月開始反應。2Q26 毛
利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所致，預估 3Q26 漲價
效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛
利率為 34.3%/35.6%，分別年增 5.5/1.3 個百分點 ，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，
+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看
好在交期不斷拉長的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。 (上一波 2021-
2022 年上升循環中毛利率由 31%迅速拉升至 37-38%)

3. 預期景氣將延續至明年，目前評價偏低 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產的精密線性傳動
元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備，在半導體產業 CapEx 確立的情況下，預期這波
拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度
EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給
予 35x 本益比，目標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

iPhone 18: Real Upgrade Cycle,

Exhibit                                                                              1                        :                    Summary of changes
Share prices as at: Sep 7, 2026.  Source: FactSet, Morgan Stanley Research
estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

Exhibit 5: Base Case –  Potential Positive Impact from FAU New Business for Largan
2026E 2027E 2028E

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives intrinsic value of NT$600/share for

materially to NT$910, but that still implies 14% downside from the latest closing price.
3. Sunny: Apple revenue to double in 2026, but positive is
well known
We believe Sunny Optical will benefit from the new iPhone launch because:
1. The company is a key vendor of  variable aperture actuators – a key spec upgrade

### Page 8

M
Idea
8
with higher ASP.
2. Sunny is expanding the product mix from a single category to multiple categories,
which could generate higher revenue from the key client.
3. Sunny is also gaining share in high-end lens products.
These drivers collectively underpin management's guidance of doubling  Apple revenue for
the full-year of 2026.
However, the fundamental performance is in line with management's guidance, so there is
lack of potential positive surprise, in our view. We therefore raise our net income

estimates 29.6% for 2026 and 26.5% for 2027 based on upbeat 1H26 results.
Consequently, we raise our price target to HK$75 but maintain our EW rating –  the strong
growth from Apple has been well expected.
4. Crystal: Prisms, coated filters, and cover lenses to benefit
We expect Crystal Optech to benefit from Apple's new product launch because  the
company is supplying three key products in the supply chain:
1. Its micro prism modules are a key enabling component for periscope (folded-

optics) telephoto cameras in smartphones. The company's prism technology uses a
folded optical path to achieve long-focal-length telephoto capability without
increasing device thickness. In 1H26, the micro prism module business enjoyed
significant volume growth. The iPhone 18 launch will directly extend this demand
into 2H26.
2. Crystal-Optech's coated filter business — which brings micro-nano optical
processing into the IRCF manufacturing process to significantly improve petal

ghosting and chromatic aberration in special imaging scenarios — is another key
segment that benefits from the iPhone 18 launch. Apple's high-end Pro models are
the primary application for this product, so the company is highly likely to
generate rapid revenue and earnings growth.
3. The iPhone 18 Pro models are expected to feature advances in 3D sensing,
including a smaller Dynamic Island as some Face ID components move beneath the
display, which sustains demand for these optical components. Crystal Optech is

well positioned to capture the growth opportunity in iPhone's 3D sensing and
cover lenses.
The company's 2Q26 results were a bit weaker than we expected because of rising costs
from R&D and capacity expansion related to new products. However, we believe this
investment could lead to stronger growth in 2028 and onward. As a result, we trim our
earnings estimates 15.6% for 2026 and 2.9% for 2027 but raise our 2028 estimate 8.2%.
Consequently, we raise our price target  7%, to Rmb32, implying 24% upside from the

current level.
Luxshare: Apple opportunity underappreciated,  resilient
growth intact
We believe Luxshare's Apple opportunity is underappreciated –  some investors worry
about the supply chain relocation. However, the company is one of Apple's most deeply

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
embedded manufacturing partners, covering various products and components, as
illustrated in the chart below. The company has  also proved its operational resilience,
consistently registering positive growth in the Apple business.
Exhibit 7: Luxshare: One of the most deeply embedded manufacturing partners for Apple
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect Luxshare to benefit from the latest new product  launch through the following

four key drivers:
1.  Second largest assembler for iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max — the most premium-heavy
iPhone lineup in history. If the  Pro/Pro Max models can take 70%+ of total iPhone
volume, it could mark the highest high-end model contribution over the past
several model cycles. A higher share from high-end models implies better revenue
and margin outlook for Luxshare.
2.   Capturing component opportunity in new  variable aperture actuator supply.

Instead of doing assembly, Lxushare is also playing the role  of supplier of actuator
mechanisms for the variable aperture camera system in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro
Max. This helps Luxshare gain exposure to high-end component business, which
carries higher margin than assembly business.
3. Foldable iPhone (iPhone Ultra) exposure: While Luxshare is not the assembler for
the foldable phone, it has actively developed foldable hinge technology, with R&D

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
focused on high-precision, long-life, large-angle hinge modules for inward-folding
flexible-display devices. The company is also developing ultra-thin haptic motors
tailored for foldable smartphones. When foldable shipments reach a certain scale,
Luxshare is also highly likely to become the second assembler.
4.  Leading AirPods assembly share:  In the AirPods assembly market, we expect
Luxshare to remain the largest supplier, with GoerTek as the second player. The

iPhone 18 launch is accompanied by new AirPods models, and Luxshare's dominant
AirPods assembly position means that it captures the full breadth of Apple's 2H26
product cycle — not just the iPhone.
Despite a significant FX loss, Luxshare posted resilient earnings in 1H26. We nudge our
EPS estimates up 0.2% each for 2026 and 2027 and 2.0% for 2028 to reflect the solid
1H26 results and positive growth outlook in the following 2-3 years. As a result, we also

raise our H-share price target to HK$82/share, implying 34% upside potential.
Lingyi: Peak season to drive strong earnings improvement
Lingyi  provides a broad range of components, including core precision parts, hinge
precision structural components, screen support plates, flanged ultra-thin stainless steel
battery case modules, etc. Following the launch of iPhone 18 Pro, Pro Max, and Apple's
first-ever foldable iPhone, Lingyi is likely to benefit from  higher content per unit on

standard Pro models, and a step-change in content value from the foldable iPhone.
Exhibit 8: Lingyi: Higher content value from new iPhone
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
Lingyi also has good exposure to the iPhone  foldable product, with content value per
foldable device expected by the company to reach US$80–90, including titanium inner
hinges, hinge components, and mid-frame support structures — substantially above the
content value of conventional smartphones.

Considering the positive drivers in 3Q26, we expect Lingyi to achieve significant earnings
improvement on a QoQ basis, which could become a key re-rating catalyst.
We slightly adjust our net income  estimates after 1H26: up 1.6%  in 2026, 0.3% and 1.7% in
2027 and 2028. However, our price targets for both the H- and A-shares are unchanged
because the revision is minor while our long-term RI model assumptions remain

unchanged.
AAC: Key beneficiary of  iPhone VC application
AAC Tech is one of Apple's key component suppliers, providing a broad portfolio spanning
acoustics, haptics, and thermal management. The iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Ultra
launch is a direct, multi-dimensional catalyst across all those business segments.
Within the three segments, we believe thermal management –  Vapor Chamber (VC) Heat
Dissipation – is the most important catalyst. AAC Technologies is a key vendor of VC heat

spreaders in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max. The significant expansion of the VC cooling
area is driven by the iPhone 18 Pro's A20 Pro chip, which debuts on a 2nm process node
with complex WMCM packaging technology to support on-device AI computation, leading
to increased thermal output. Apple first introduced VC liquid cooling in the iPhone 17 Pro
series, with AAC as the primary supplier for that generation as well.
This division has already demonstrated significant growth momentum heading into the

iPhone 18 ramp up. In 1H26, heat dissipation revenue surged approximately 400% YoY, to
RMB1.10 billion, primarily driven by the continued ramp-up in shipments of high-
performance innovative vapor chambers. The company expects VC shipments to exceed
180,000k units in 2026, up from 140,000k+  units in 2025.

Combining the strong growth momentum in thermal management, but weaker-than-

investors are aware of the weakness in optics, while the iPhone launch could help re-rate

the stock. We raise our price target to HK$50 – the stronger 19% increase reflects
earnings upgrades and a higher valuation for the thermal-management business – and
maintain our OW rating.
BYDE: Normal peak season, but lackluster with limited spec
upgrade

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
While Apple is BYDE's key customer, accounting for >50% of total revenue, it is mainly
from the  iPad assembly business. Therefore, this division is less likely to benefit from the
iPhone 18 launch.
The company is also the second-largest iPhone metal casing vendor, supplying precision
structural components including titanium and aluminium alloy chassis. While iPhone 18
launch is the direct driver for this segment, the titanium-to-aluminium downgrade has

become a near-term headwind.  Titanium is a higher-value, higher-margin material for BYDE
while aluminium carries lower ASP and lower gross margin. This change has made BYDE
less likely to benefit from the iPhone 18 launch.
Despite the lackluster outlook, we expect earnings to show sequential improvement in
3Q26, driven by the normal peak season in 3Q26, followed by stronger growth in 4Q26 as
liquid cooling products for AI infrastructure also ramp up. Our earnings estimates and

price target remain unchanged.
GoerTek: Margin might face pressures despite peak season
GoerTek's primary Apple exposure is in smart acoustic complete units (AirPods assembly
and components) and precision components. We believe the  iPhone 18 launch and peak
season ramp in 3Q–4Q will drive higher shipment volumes for these products.
However, GoerTek's transfer of production  to Vietnam — where it has invested
approximately US$1.3 billion through multiple entities — has created a persistent labor

cost headwind. In 2H25, a labor shortage caused by the rapid capacity transfer forced
GoerTek to offer higher wages, triggering material  gross margin pressure  for the smart
acoustic assembly business. Overall gross margins partially recovered to 13.8% in 1Q26
and 15.2% in 2Q26, but the underlying trend of rising wages in Vietnam remains a
structural factor. As GoerTek ramps production for the iPhone 18 peak season in 3Q and
4Q, the higher labor cost base in Vietnam will continue to weigh on assembly margins.

We raise our 2026 and 2027 earnings estimates 14.9% and 10.6%, respectively, mainly
reflecting the better-than-expected gross margin recovery in 1H26. However, we raise our
price target only modestly: 4%. to Rmb27, as persistent labor-cost pressure in Vietnam
could limit further margin expansion and reduce the earnings benefit from higher peak-
season volumes.
Considering the factors discussed above, we believe GoerTek is less likely to benefit from

the new iPhone product launch and peak season volume increase.

Where we could be wrong
While we expect iPhone 18 to be  a positive catalyst in 2H26, we are watching three key
variables on the potential inflection points:
1.  Memory prices: These are a key variable this year. If there is a memory price
correction anytime during 2026, investors are likely to expect an earlier
smartphone shipment and margin recovery, which could reverse the negative

sentiment.
2.  LLMs: We see LLMs as a revolutionary innovation. They could introduce numerous
creative features in smartphone applications and change user habits in the years to
come. If the Edge AI breakthrough arrives earlier than we expect, it could
accelerate the smartphone product cycle and make the recovery earlier.
3. iPhone sales performance: Better-than-expected sell-through of the iPhone 18
Pro, Pro Max and its first foldable model could drive shipment upside, higher

content value and stronger capacity utilization. Conversely, weaker demand could
lead to order cuts after the initial ramp up and reduce the earnings benefits from
key hardware upgrades.

Price target changes
We derive our price targets (also our base case values) using a residual income model –
the same methodology we use for our Greater China Tech coverage.
Our new price targets are derived from revised earnings estimates and unchanged cost of
equity assumptions (RFR, beta, risk premium) and growth rates.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

穩懋 (3105 TT) 08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ，符合群益先前預估， 3Q26 營
收動能來自 optical 、 infrastructure ， 預 估 09/2026 營 收 仍 將 較 08/2026 增加 。 就評價
而言，穩懋 受 惠 於 PIN 等 光 通 訊 新 訂 單 自 06/2026 開 始 量 產 ， 預 估 出 貨 量 4Q26 將持
續放大， 且 後 續 成 長 空 間 可 期，為 4Q26 、 2027 年重要營收動能，故群益 維 持 對 穩 懋

cj.chen@capital.com.tw

08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ：  
穩懋 08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ， 符 合 群 益 先 前 預 估 ，3Q26 營
收 動 能 來 自 optical 、infrastructure ，預 估 09/2026 營 收 仍 將 較 08/2026 增 加。
在 穩 懋 各 主 要 產 品 中，optical 產 品 受 惠 於 PIN 等 光 通 訊 新 訂 單 出 貨 量 放 大，
營 收 成 長 性 居 各 產 品 之 冠；其 次，infrastructure 產 品 出 貨 動 能 來 自 低 軌 道 衛
星標案陸續出貨，由於全球各地低軌道衛星標案隨應用領域拓展而有機會
持 續 釋 出 ， 後 續 營 收 前 景 亦 仍 佳 。 
就 評 價 而 言，穩 懋 受 惠 於 PIN 等 光 通 訊 新 訂 單 自 06/2026 開 始 量 產，預 估 出
貨量 4Q26 將 持 續 放 大 ， 且 後 續 成 長 空 間 可 期 ， 為 4Q26 、 2027 年 重 要 營 收
動 能 ， 故 群 益 維 持 對 穩 懋 Buy 的 投 資 建 議

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 8 日

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

 Lily.tsai@ctbcsis.com

隱形眼鏡  
望隼 (4771 TT)

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
訂單需求持續強勁 ， 加快擴產速度  
⚫ 預期 2026 下半 年營收將逐季墊高  
⚫ 中國大陸強 勁成長， 2026 下半年轉 由日本驅動成長  
⚫ 產能持續擴 充 ，2026 年底 產能將 提升至 8,400 萬片  
預期 2026 下半年營收將逐季墊高 ：望隼 2Q26 營運表現強勁， 營收
12.0 億元 (QoQ+ 16%、YoY+37%)；毛利率 43.5%，較去年同期提升 2
個百分點，主要受惠稼動率滿載產生的規模經濟，以及製程持續優
化；帶動 營業利益率 提升至 31%，稅後淨利 2.8 億元 (YoY+86%)、EPS 
4.7 3 元。 展望 2026 下半年， 公司表示日本及中國大陸客戶訂單持續
回流，加上節慶備貨需求、矽水膠產品開始明顯 放量 ，以及新產能逐
步開出，營收有望逐季成長；毛利率則有望受惠稼動率持續高檔 及產
品組合優化 ，持穩於上半年的高水準 42~43% 。 
中國大陸強勁成長， 2026 下半年 由日本接棒 ：區域營運方面，中國大
陸佔比 54% 為主要營收來源 ，受惠定位彩片 引發的熱潮 ，前 7 月營收
YoY+55% 。日本市場佔比 35% ，雖因產能優先支援中國而 YoY-9%，
但隨著下半年產能轉向全力供應日本，預期業績將迎來強勁成長。台
灣市場受惠於 通路策略與 產品口碑發酵，營收 YoY+148% 。美國市場
則為長線佈局重點，公司持續積極推廣矽水膠產品，預計到 2027 年
下半年將有 較顯著客戶進 展，擴大整體海外市場。 公司 未來將結合多
焦、散光、抗藍光 等功能 於矽水膠 材質，持續 以差異化產品 擴大市佔 。 
產能持續擴充 ，2026 年底將來到 8,400 萬片 ：因應持續湧現的市場訂
單，望隼積極展開產能擴充計畫 。目前月產能近 7,000 萬片，稼動率
維持在高檔；並持續進行擴產工作， 預計至 2026 年底月產能將提升
至 8,400 萬片。 2027 年則以 1 億片為目標， 但具體擴產時間點仍會觀
察訂單和需求來決定 。產品組合 部分 ，2Q26 美瞳片佔比來到 58% ，
市 場對美 瞳片 需求持 續強 勁；矽 水膠 佔比 約 2%，已 開始出 貨日本 市
場，預期 2026 下半年在配合日本客戶新產品的上市，出貨量將進一
步放大，帶動矽水膠 全年營收佔比至 3~5% 。 
評價：以未來四季 預估 EPS 計算，給予 近年本益比區間中 緣 12.5 倍，
目標價 235 元，維持增加持股評等。 風險因子 ：矽水膠推廣不如預期 、
中國市場消費景氣變化、 匯率波動 。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

朋程 (8255 TT)  Neutral

全球車市展望平淡，營運成長溫和，投資評等為 Neutral 
投資建議 ：基於： (1)2Q26 營收成長不如預期，主因歐洲客戶拉
貨放緩。 (2)2H26 因原物料及晶圓代工成本上揚，使獲利持續壓
抑。 (3)  全球車市展望復甦緩慢，加上中國車市內捲嚴重，不利
於朋程評價提升，投資評等為 Neutral 。  
歐洲客戶拉貨放緩， 2Q26 本業獲利 YOY 下滑 ：朋程 2Q26 營
收 20.32 億元 (QOQ-0.3%/YOY-6.1%) ，不如市場預期，主因受歐
洲客戶拉貨放緩，使 48V MOSFET 模組出貨量顯著放緩，毛利
率 26.98% ，與前季維持相當；費用率 20.60% ，與前季一致，本
業獲利 1.3 億元， YOY 下滑 44.5% ，惟業外方面，因 25 年受匯
損低基期影響，稅後淨利為 1.46 億元 (QOQ-
15.4%/YOY+276.3%) ，稅後 EPS 1.44 元。  
預估 2H26 營運逐季增溫，惟原物料及代工成本上揚，壓抑獲
利：展望 2H26 ，受惠超效能二極體 (ULLD) 訂單需求暢旺，加上
高毛利客製化 IGBT 之工業產品於 4Q26 開始小量量產，預估朋
程 2H26 營運有望呈現溫和增溫的態勢， 3Q26 及 4Q26 營收分
別有望季增 3.7% 及 5.1%，然而毛利率方面，因受原物料銅價上
揚及上游晶圓代工漲價影響，使 2H26 毛利率有所壓抑，研究部
預估較 1H26 平均毛利率約衰退 1.2pt 。預估朋程 2026 年營收為
83.88 億元 (YOY+3.5%) ，毛 利 率26.35%(YOY-0.4%pt) ，稅 後EPS 
6.06 元 (YOY-4%) ，獲利較 2025 年呈微幅衰退。  
展望 2027 年， ULLD 動能續強，靜待工業新品實質貢獻： 展望
2027 年，因應歐美碳排法規趨嚴，車廠追求高 CP 值解決方案，
進而推升 ULLD 相關需求，抵銷傳統二極體及 LLD 需求下滑影
響。而 SiC 方面，項目尚在送樣驗證階段，靜待實質貢獻發酵。
然毛利率則預期因晶圓代工及原物料成本持續上揚，將抵銷產
品組合轉佳影響，預估朋程 2027 年毛利率將呈持穩態勢。綜上
所述 ， 研究部預估朋程2027 年營收為 93.06 億元 (YOY+10.9%) ，
毛利率 26.53% ，稅後 EPS 7.29 元 (YOY+20.3%) 。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：  
朋程科技身為全球車用二極體與功率模組龍頭，將 ESG 視為企業永續的核
心，積極推動「綠色製造、誠信治理、社會共好」三大面向。  
一、環境保護（ Environmental ）：  
碳管理與淨零路徑：遵循 ISO 14064-1 完成全廠區溫室氣體盤查，推動 ISO 
50001 能源管理系統，並訂定 2050 淨零排放（ Net Zero ）長期目標。綠色產
品與節能低碳：主力產品「超高效率二極體（ LL-DIODE ）」顯著提升發電
機效率，協助全球燃油車及混動車降低二氧化碳排放。積極佈局 SiC（碳化
矽）與 IGBT 功率模組，推動電動車（ EV）與綠能逆變器高能效轉型。綠色
製造與資源循環：導入廢棄物資源化與節水設備，達成 ISO 14001 與 ISO 
45001 認證，落實有害物質禁用 (RoHS/REACH 規範 )。  
二、社會責任（ Social ）：  
人本關懷與職業安全：打造多元平等職場（ DEI ），提供完善薪酬福利與育
兒支持；連續多年獲得職業安全衛生優良單位人才培育與技術傳承：建立產
學合作計畫，補助員工持續教育，重視車用半導體研發人才之長期留任。  
供應鏈永續管理：制定《供應商行為準則》，要求供應商簽署不使用衝突礦
石（ Conflict-Free Minerals ）聲明，落實供應鏈環境與人權評核。社會參與
與公益：長期投入偏鄉教育、在地社區關懷與綠色植樹活動，回饋營運據點
周邊社區。  
三、公司治理（ Governance ）：  
誠信經營與資訊透明：設有獨立董事與審計委員會，定期進行板塊運作與董
事會績效評估 ， 保障股東與利益關係人權益 。 資訊安全與永續報告 ： 取得ISO 
27001 資訊安全管理認證，定期發布符合 GRI 準則與 SASB 規範之 ESG 永
續報告書。風險控管與韌性：建立地緣政治、氣候變遷風險（ TCFD ）與供
應鏈中斷之營運持續計畫（ BCP ）。

3 群益投顧 
公司簡介：  
朋程 (8255 TT) 成立於 1998 年，總部位於桃園市蘆竹區，是一家車用二極體
與功率半導體廠，為全球汽車發電機整流二極體最大供應商。公司目前業務
與產品分為四大核心領域， 1.一般整流二極體 (Standard Diode) ：主要應用於
傳統燃油車發電機，提供基本整流與車用電源轉化功能； 2.高效能二极體
(LLD / ULLD) ：包含高效能與超高效能二極體，具備低功耗與高轉換效率特
性，以因應各國車輛碳排放與節能法規要求； 3.新能源車功率模組 (EV Power 
Module) ：專注於油電混合動力車之逆變器、主驅動模組，並透過旗下子公
司安捷特布局矽 (Si) 與第三代半導體碳化矽 (SiC) 、氮化鎵 (GaN) 晶片設計與
封裝； 4.工控與特殊應用 (Industrial & Others) ：包含工業用 IGBT 模組、 AI
伺服器電源管理及儲能系統功率半導體解決方案。 2026 年銷售區域以亞洲
市場 (含中國大陸 )為主要營收來源，北美與歐洲次之。 2025 年營收產品
別 :Diode 佔 29% 、 LLD 佔 28% 、 ULLD 佔 21% 、 xEV 佔 17% 。

歐洲客戶拉貨放緩， 2Q26 本業獲利 YOY 下滑：  
朋程 2Q26 營收 20.32 億元 (QOQ-0.3%/YOY-6.1%) ，不如市場預期，主因受
歐洲客戶拉貨放緩，使 48V MOSFET 模組出貨量顯著放緩，毛利率 26.98% ，
與前季維持相當，費用率 20.60% ，與前季一致，本業獲利 1.3 億元， YOY
下滑 44.5% ，惟業外方面，因 25 年受匯損低基期影響，稅後淨利為 1.46 億
元 (QOQ-15.4%/YOY+276.3%) ，稅後 EPS 1.44 元。

預估 2H26 營運逐季增溫，惟原物料及代工成本上揚，壓抑獲利：  
展望 2H26 ，受惠超效能二極體 (ULLD) 訂單需求暢旺，加上高毛利客製化
IGBT 之工業產品於 4Q26 開始小量量產，預估朋程 2H26 營運有望呈現溫
和增溫的態勢， 3Q26 及 4Q26 營收分別有望季增 3.7% 及 5.1% ，然而毛利率
方面，因受原物料銅價上揚及上游晶圓代工漲價影響，使 2H26 毛利率有所
壓抑，研究部預估較 1H26 平均毛利率約衰退 1.2pt 。整體產能預估方面，
Diode( 一般二極體 )約 1.3 億 ~1.4 億顆， LLD( 低功耗二極體 )7200 萬 ~7500 萬
顆， ULLD( 超高效二極體 )4000 萬 ~4200 萬顆， xEV( 新能源車模組 )接近 300
萬顆。綜上所述，預估朋程 2026 年營收為 83.88 億元 (YOY+3.5%) ，毛利率
26.35%(YOY-0.4%pt) ，稅後淨利為 6.17 億元 (YOY-4%) ，稅後 EPS 6.06 元，
獲利較 2025 年呈微幅衰退。

展望 2027 年， ULLD 接單動能續強，靜待工業事業發酵：  
展望 2027 年，因各國碳排法規越來越嚴 格，車廠需要降低燃油車的油耗與
廢氣。 ULLD 可以直接替換傳統整流二極體，提升發電機效率、降低發熱量
與碳排，不需要車廠大幅改動整車電氣架構，為成本最低的減碳方案，使目
前 ULLD 接單動能續強。 ULLD 單價是傳統二極體的達 6 倍，在產線滿載
與出貨增長的帶動下，可有效填補傳統二極體及 LLD 下滑影響。此外，新
產品方面，公司表示， IGBT 新產能將於 4Q26 起開始放量，單月出貨量將
達 150K ，並預計於 1Q27 單季出貨量將從 500K 逐季提升至 800K ，此項產
品高於公司平均毛利率。而 SIC 方面，項目尚在送樣驗證階段，將聚焦 AI、

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Document 206: Consumer Morsel_ Energy Bills_ Short-term relief, longer-term challenges Utility bill inflation has moderated, but household payments remain elevated and longer-term pressures are

Source PDF: all report pdf/Consumer Morsel_ Energy Bills_ Short-term relief, longer-term challenges Utility bill inflation has moderated, but household payments remain elevated and longer-term pressures are building. 2 min read.pdf

Economy 
Energy bills: Short-term relief, longer-term challenges 
16 September 2026

Key takeaways 
• Energy bill inflation was around 4% year-over-year (YoY) in August 2026. However, Bank of America internal deposit data shows 
average utility bill payments rose faster, increasing 5% YoY from June through August. This was likely due, in part, to higher 
cooling demand during a warm summer. 
• But bill pressures remain highly uneven across the country. Average utility payments have risen by more than 10% YoY in cities 
such as Detroit, Baltimore and Washington DC, while consumers in Orlando, San Jose and Tampa have seen declines. Some of 
these regional differences appear to reflect policy and infrastructure factors as much as energy usage itself. 
• The long-term outlook still points to upward pressure on electricity bills as demand from data center construction, electrification 
and broader economic activity drive the need for additional generation and grid investment. However, a forecasted strong El 
Niño event this winter could provide some near-term relief, if warmer-than-normal temperatures reduce household heating 
demand, partially offsetting structural cost pressures.

Energy bill inflation has moderated  
Energy is an essential household expense. According to Bureau of Labor Statistics data, energy bills accounted for roughly 3% of 
total household spending as of 2024. US utility prices have been far less affected by higher global oil prices stemming from the 
conflict in the Middle East than gasoline prices, largely because the US is a net exporter of natural gas. Nonetheless, the cost of 
energy has been rising for a prolonged period: since January 2024 the price of electricity has risen 10% and the price of piped 
gas is up 18%.  
The better news is that utility price inflation has moderated relative to the second half of 2025 and early 2026. As of August, 
electricity and piped gas prices were up around 4% year-over-year (YoY). But Bank of America internal deposit data shows that 
average utility payments for electricity and gas were running ahead of inflation in these categories, up 5.3% YoY. One reason 
could be that household demand for cooling was strong this summer, due to relatively warm weather (Exhibit 1).  
Exhibit 1: Utility bill payments rose around 5.3% YoY in August  
Consumer prices for electricity and piped gas (% YoY) and three-month average monthly utility payments per customer based on Bank of America internal 
deposit data (% YoY)

Source: Haver Analytics, Bank of America internal data

Energy costs vary widely by region 
While energy bills account for a relatively modest share of household spending nationally, experiences vary across the country. 
Looking at large cities (by Core Based Statistical Areas), our data shows that utility bills rose by more than 10% YoY in Detroit, 
Baltimore and Washington DC from June through August 2026. At the other end of the spectrum, bills fell YoY in Orlando, San 
Joe and Tampa (Exhibit 2).  
Customers’ bills are being pressured by reshoring, reindustrialization, the general pass-through costs in the economy, and a 
significant need for infrastructure replacement in many markets. Regulatory changes and one-off charges have also influenced 
costs in several areas. In Florida, for example, the removal of hurricane recovery surcharges reduced utility bills in 2026, while in 
the Northeast, investments in grid infrastructure and capacity have contributed to higher costs.  
There also remains some disparities in where average bills sit relative to the overall US (Exhibit 3). In Washington DC, Boston, 
Phoenix and Dallas, the average utility bill payment in June through August 2026 was well above the US average. Conversely, 
Chicago, Nashville and Las Vegas paid some of the lowest energy bills relative to the US.

Rising demand for electricity will continue to pressure bills  
Looking longer-term, rising energy demand (particularly for electricity), driven by factors such as data center expansion and 
residential electrification, is likely to keep upward pressure on consumers’ energy bills for a number of years (read Is the AI 
revolution raising consumers' utility bills? and Can the AI buildout keep building? for more).  
Exhibit 4 shows forecasts from the US Energy Information Administration’s August 2026 Short-term Energy Outlook, estimating 
a 4% YoY rise in commercial and industrial electricity consumption. Accommodating this growth will likely require ongoing 
investment in grid capacity and power generation, and some of these costs may be passed on to consumers through higher 
energy bills.  
Exhibit 4: The demand for electricity is rising particularly from commercial and industrial sectors 
US electrical demand (in billion kilowatt hours) 2020-2027E (estimates/forecast from 2026)

Source: US Energy Information Administration 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Short-term, the weather could prove important  
El Niño is the warming phase of ENSO (El Niño–Southern Oscillation), a global climate phenomenon that emerges from variation 
in winds and sea surface temperatures over the Pacific Ocean. El Niño events can have widespread and profound global impacts, 
causing extreme weather such as storms, floods and higher temperatures. The strongest recorded El Niño took place in 1877 to 
1878 and led to a global famine that killed more than 50 million people.  
The National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) believes there is a high probability that a very strong El Niño 
event will occur this year (Exhibit 5). One way to put this in historical context is through the NOAA’s Relative Oceanic Niño Index 
(RONI). A positive RONI indicates the central/eastern tropical Pacific is unusually warm relative to the rest of the tropics. A 
higher RONI indicates a more severe El Niño, though NOAA points out that uncertainty on impacts also tends to rise with RONI.  
Exhibit 5: The NOAA attaches a very high probability to a severe El Niño event over coming months  
Likelihood of an El Nino event estimated by severity (%)

Source: National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA)

Exhibit 6 shows that the current forecast for RONI from October to December 2026 is one of the largest on record. In recent 
history, similar-sized El Niño events have only occurred in 1997-98, 2015-16 and to a lesser extent in 2022-23.  
Exhibit 6: The El Niño event in 2026 is forecasted to be the largest on record 
Relative Oceanic Niño Index (RONI) for October-December by year (2026: NOAA 50% probability forecast for October-December 2026)

Source: National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) 
Note: Red bars: 1997-1998/2015-16. Yellow bar: 2026-27 50% forecast. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
For the US, significant El Niño events tend to bring higher-than-usual temperatures and more rainfall (Exhibit 7). However, no El 
Niño is exactly the same. For example, in 1997-98 California was flooded in but in 2015-16 it was dry, while the nation overall 
was wet. Both time periods were among the highest levels of RONI in the past 70 years.  
Exhibit 7: El Niño events have been noted for higher temperatures and rainfall

I NSTI TUTE

What does this mean for consumers and their energy bills? The most predictable impact from a severe El Niño appears to be 
unseasonably warm temperatures, implying reduced heating needs during the fall and winter. Consumers could therefore receive 
some relief in their utility bills and potentially have additional income to spend on discretionary goods and services over the 
coming months.  
There is a large caveat, however. If El Niño manifests in stormy and rainy conditions – likely impacting the southern parts of the 
US – then some households may end up having to spend on repairs and maintenance following weather damage (read more on 
consumer spending in the aftermath of natural disasters in October’s What's the impact of natural disasters on local 
economies?). This might boost consumer discretionary spending, but not in a way that implies improved well-being. And longer 
term, the broader impact of both climate change and energy demands from the AI revolution are likely to keep upward pressure 
on bills.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts.  
Durables spending is defined as spending on electronics, building materials, auto-related spending and furniture. Premium durables spending is 
based on a selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively higher value products. Conversely, value durables spending is based on a 
selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively lower value products.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Charts of the week: Contained bond volatility, robust earnings

As long as US bond market volatility remains contained, the equity rally is likely to continue amid robust earnings growth  
S&P500 index, US bond market volatility (inverted)    Consensus estimates for S&P500 earnings and revenue growth

Source: Bloomberg, LSEG I/B/E/S, Standard Chartered

Editorial 
Rising risk of a Fed rate hike 
Strategy summary: Fed Chair Warsh has raised the chance of 
a rate hike this year, potentially as early as this month if August 
payrolls remain resilient and if there is no clear sign of 
disinflation. However, we see little scope for multiple hikes, 
given our view that disinflation is likely to continue into next year 
as the impact of tariffs and high oil prices fade. Thus, any such 
‘defensive’ rate hike is likely to be neutral for equity and bond 
markets which are already partly anticipating one.   
We would adopt a ‘buy on dips’ strategy for equities on any 
seasonal or pre-election volatility spike amid a robust earnings 
outlook. In bonds, we gradually extend the maturity profile to 3-
7 years as yields in this segment have risen to attractive levels.   
Setting the stage for a Fed hike: At Jackson Hole, Fed Chair 
Warsh opened the door to a potential rate hike, noting that rates 
are not restrictive, employment remains near full and underlying 
inflation is still elevated. We see just over 50% chance of a rate 
hike, potentially on 16 September, if key US data before then 
are supportive – specifically, if August payrolls are positive, 
unemployment stays near 4.1% and core inflation is at least 
0.2% m/m. If these conditions are met, not hiking could trigger 
another long- end bond selloff and undermine Warsh’s 
credibility. Meanwhile, a rate hike under these conditions is 
likely to have limited market impact, as money markets are 
already pricing over 50% chance of September hike. 
Alternative scenario: Conversely, another month of negative 
payrolls in August or core inflation cooling below 0.2% m/m 
would allow neutral -to-dovish Fed members to push back 
against a rate hike this month. In that case, December would 
be the next stop for reassessment ; a rate hike in October, 
weeks before the November mid-term elections, is unlikely. 
Little scope for multiple hikes:  Even if the Fed delivers a 
‘defensive’ hike this year, we see little scope for further 
increases as US inflation should ease towards the 2% target 
next year when tariff and oil-price effects fade. New York Fed 
President Williams and Governor Waller’s refusal to pre-commit 
to a September or a future hike underscores the disinflationary 
outlook. Also, multiple rate rises could prove counterproductive 
by lifting already record US government borrowing costs as the 
Treasury continues to shorten maturities of its bond issues. 
Gradually extend maturity in bonds: The latest rise in short-
to-medium term bond yields is thus an opportunity to gradually 
extend maturities in bonds, given our outlook for disinflation and 
limited upside for policy rates. We extend our preferred maturity 
bucket to 3-7 years from 3- 5 years. We continue to prefer 
corporate bonds, where we are comfortable in going down to 
BB-rated USD-denominated credit, which offer 6.5-7.5% yield. 
Buying any September dip in equities. Rising bond yields , 
seasonal weakness in September and pre- midterm election 
blues are potential near-term headwinds for equities. However, 
equities have historically held up well despite higher bond yields 
as long as bond volatility is contained and growth resilient.  
In fact, history suggests taking advantage of any weakness in 
September to build long-term exposure as underlying earnings 
growth prospects remain solid (20%-plus S&P500 EPS growth 
expected in Q3 and Q4). For instance, over the past 15 years, 
buying the S&P 500 index at its September low has generated 
an average return of 11.4% over the subsequent six months. If 
we focus only on years when September ended in negative 
territory, the average six-month return rises to 15.1%.  
ECB to hike again next week. While a Fed rate hike remains 
a toss-up, the ECB is highly likely to hike 25bps to 2.5% next 
week. Euro area Q2 GDP expanded an above-trend 0.4% q/q, 
as the region weathered the energy shock better than feared. 
The momentum has carried into Q3. August headline inflation 
climbed to a near-three-year-high of 3.3% y/y on energy prices, 
although core inflation eased to 2.4% as second-round effects 
remain absent. With no progress seen in resolving the Middle 
East conflict, the case for ECB taking further pre-emptive action 
has risen. We remain focused on whether President Lagarde 
pushes back against market expectations of further rate hikes.    
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

Top client questions 
  F ollowing Fed Chair Warsh’s debut keynote address at  
Jackson Hole, do you expect a Fed rate hike this month? 
Our view: We  have raised our probability of a September Fed 
rate hike following Fed Chair Warsh’s Jackson Hole speech, as 
we believe the bar for keeping rates unchanged is now high. 
Rationale: The Fed’s price-stability mandate will be put to the test  
at its  16 September meeting. Fed Chair Warsh is expected to 
demonstrate his  inflation-fighting resolve, consistent with his 
Jackson Hole remarks that summer’s encouraging inflation readings 
were insufficient to confirm  a structural disinflationary trend, 
reaffirming the central bank’s focus on delivering price stability.  
Warsh’s comments lead us to believe that the bar for leaving rates 
unchanged is now high. A hold would likely require a major labour 
market deterioration and a materially softer inflation print in incoming 
data releases; in-line data would not be interpreted as dovish. 
However, in our view, a  rate hike would not mark the start of a 
sustained tightening cycle, as we expect disinflationary pressures 
and labour market weakness to persist. This view is also reflected 
in the US bond yield curve’s reaction to Warsh’s remarks. The curve 
bear-flattened, with short -term yields rising more than long-term 
yields. As yields in the sub- 10-year segment have moved higher 
relative to those in other parts of the curve, we suggest corporate 
bond investors to take advantage of the move by extending duration 
from the 3-5- year bucket to the 3-7-year bucket. We believe this 
segment is short enough to avoid the volatility and crowded 
positioning at the long end, yet long enough to benefit if the Fed 
remains on hold, given the short end of the curve is pricing in a hike. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Would the US 30-year government bond yield break above 
its 20-year high of 5.4%? 
Our view: We believe the US 30-year government bond yield is 
unlikely to remain  above 5.4% sustainably in the short term. 
However, we expect US long -end bond yields to stay elevated 
and the US 30-year bond yield to range between  5.25% and 
5.50% over the next 12 months. 
Rationale: In the near term, with the US Treasury’s long-end bond 
buyback programme in place, we expect temporary moves above 
5.4%, but these are unlikely to be sustained.  
In the longer term, the effectiveness of the buyback programme is 
questionable given structural headwinds – a deteriorating US fiscal 
trajectory, waning foreign demand, AI -driven corporate supply and 
sticky inflation amplified by the Middle East conflict – which argue for 
yields remaining elevated even if the buyback program me limits 
the most acute spikes. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Taiwan is one of the few tech -heavy equity markets that 
recovered the ground lost in July. Is it time to take profit or add? 
Our view: We remain constructive on Taiwan and continue to 
view it as an attractive Opportunistic idea.  
Rationale: The MSCI Taiwan Index rebounded by 4.5% in August  
after declining 3.9% in July , in TWD terms. Recent guidance from 
hyperscalers indicate s that AI infrastructure spending remains 
resilient, with few signs of a slowdown. Taiwan remains a key 
beneficiary of the AI infrastructure buildout through its leadership 
in semiconductor manufacturing and the AI hardware ecosystem. 
Beyond foundries, many Taiwan companies are critical suppliers  
of printed circuit boards, liquid cooling and networking solutions, all 
of which benefit from rising AI hardware complexity and deployment.  
Although investors have raised concerns around circular financing, 
we view this largely as a reflection of AI's capital-intensive nature, 
in which incumbent technology firms provide funding and strategic 
support to ecosystem partners to accelerate product development  
and capacity expansion. While MSCI Taiwan’s price- to-earnings 
(P/E) ratio remains above its five-year average, valuations have 
moderated from June’s peak following the recent correction. This is 
supported by a stronger growth outlook  (2025-28E compound 
annual growth rate of 40.4% vs. 5.0% in 2021-25). As such , we 
remain constructive on Taiwan and its pivotal role in the global AI 
manufacturing ecosystem.   
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
 Amid rising commodity prices, do you see  any 
opportunities within energy and commodity equities? 
Our view: We favour maintaining energy and commodity sector 
allocations closer to long term strategic benchmark weights  
rather than taking a significant Overweight position.  
Rationale: While there may be selective opportunities within the 
energy sector amid rising commodity prices, we favour maintaining 
allocations closer to strategic benchmark weights rather than 
taking a significant Overweight position. The binary nature of the 
near-term oil price outlook and any changes in geopolitical relations 
can create significant volatility. We also expect oil prices to ease 
towards USD 70/bbl over the next 12 months as transit conditions 
normalise, alongside a sizeable supply surplus expected in 2027. 
While i nvestors should not ignore energy stocks from a portfolio 
construction perspective , we continue to favo ur sectors where 
earnings visibility is stronger , such as t echnology and 
financials. We also take a more selective approach within materials, 
with gold reinstated to Overweight as the price outlook has notably 
improved alongside a sharp pullback in the USD. We have raised 
our three- and 12-month gold price targets to USD 4,750/oz  and 
USD 5,000/oz, respectively.  
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Which currencies stand to benefit the most  as more 
central banks adopt a hawkish stance? 
Our view: The AUD appears best placed to benefit from further 
hawkish repricing, as additional RBA tightening remains only 
partly priced in. JPY upside risks have increased, but markets 
have already discounted substantial BoJ tightening, limiting the 
scope for a fresh policy surprise. The EUR retains some policy 
support, while NZD upside looks more limited following the 
RBNZ’s less hawkish forward guidance. 
Rationale: Currencies typically benefit most when central banks 
tighten more than markets expect. For Australia, Bloomberg pricing 
implies only around 12.7bps of additional RBA tightening, leaving 
greater scope for AUD upside if inflation or activity data prompt a 
more hawkish RBA path. 
For the JPY, recent hawkish repricing and official resistance to 
excessive weakness are supportive.  However, with substantial 
BoJ tightening already priced in, further sustained gains would  
require a faster or larger tightening path than currently discounted. 
The EUR would similarly need stronger guidance on further 
tightening to extend gains, while NZD upside looks more limited after 
the RBNZ’s projected rate path proved shallower than expected. 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
 How does the outcome of the recent G20 Finance Ministers 
and Central Bank Governors meeting affect your oil outlook? 
Our View: The lack of a diplomatic breakthrough at the  recent 
G20 meeting suggests geopolitical risk premi a will persist. As 
geopolitics remains the dominant driver of crude oil prices, we 
expect West Texas Intermediate (WTI)  oil to trade toward s the 
upper end of our USD 70-90/bbl range in the near term. 
Rationale: The  recent G20 finance track produced no movement 
towards conflict de-escalation in t he Middle East . Instead, the 
emphasis shifted towards tighter financial and economic 
pressure on Iran. While this reduces the risk of an extended military 
campaign, it points to a more prolonged period of supply 
uncertainty.  
Market estimates put total Gulf flows near 9.5 mb/d – around 50% 
of the pre- conflict norm  – supported by alternative routing and 
reported ‘dark transit s’, which are helping offset some supply 
disruptions. However, declining inventory buffers should keep WTI 
supported around USD 90/bbl. Given China is a key buyer of Iranian 
crude, the Trump-Xi summit later this month is the next major event 
to watch, as any shift in China's stance towards US pressure on Iran 
could materially affect export flows and oil prices. 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #bonds #housing #interestrates
The national accounts inkblot test
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
16:30 September 04 2026 Share
A story-driven species encounters national accounts data that resist easy narratives.
In something as complex as an economy, outcomes often defy simple narratives. It is a little like
those inkblot psychology tests one sometimes sees. Is it a rabbit or is it a lady? It depends on your

perspective.

### Page 2

In something as complex as an economy, there are always many things going on, not all pointing in the
same direction. There will be headwinds and tailwinds. Most shocks and trends affect some industries
and parts of the economy more than others. The overall impact is a complicated netting exercise that is
hard to be precise about in the real world, where measurement is uncertain and things get revised.
That complexity sits uncomfortably for a species that shapes its understanding through story. A simple

cause-and-effect narrative fits the way our brains have evolved. Messy outcomes like this week’s
national accounts (and the domestic data flow more broadly) do not fit simple stories. It is tempting for
people to extract one nonetheless. The process resembles one of those old psychology tricks where
you can see a rabbit or a woman, a face or a vase, depending on your perspective, or the Rorschach
inkblot where you can “see” what you want to see. We pattern-match, we see creatures in the clouds –

and sometimes, we can be selective with data when we should not be, focusing only on what fits our
narrative.
I am not going to pretend to be above reproach on this front, being a member of the same narrative-
seeking species as everyone else. That said, I cannot help noticing how easy it is to squint at the
economic inkblot and see a hawk or a dove according to one’s prior beliefs.
Those seeing the hawk will point to (among other things) the 2.1%yr GDP growth being above the

RBA’s forecast and not below its estimate of potential output growth “speed limit”, as the RBA is trying
to engineer. They will note the tick-up in unit labour cost growth, resilient consumption, strong data
centre investment and – beyond the national accounts – the outsized July CPI inflation data. Putting
these together, a narrative forms that seemingly demands immediate further tightening of monetary
policy.
In doing so, though, one would need to leave a few things out. Among these are the fact that growth

stepped down noticeably through the year, and that about half the upside surprise in year-ended
growth was a revision to an earlier quarter, implying you got more growth for the same amount of
inflation. Also downplayed in this narrative is the weak growth in average earnings per hour – a less-
noisy input into unit labour costs – the increase in household saving and the roll-off in public sector
investment making room for higher dwelling and data centre investment.

And for those confidently expecting a September hike, it requires a belief that the RBA will go back on
their previous statement that, in this initial phase, they will focus on the quarterly inflation data rather
The national accounts for Q2 2026 were a case in point. A hawkish take would focus on GDP growth
being above RBA expectations and not below its view of trend; a dovish take would focus on the loss
of momentum more recently. Likewise a hawk could point to the outsized July CPI print, while the

dove can remind you of the noise and uncertain seasonality in the monthly data. Both narratives
inherently leave out contradicting signals.
The key issue here is to make sure that you are taking signal from all the available data, not just the
things that fit the narrative. Labour market data deserve more consideration than some observers are
giving them. Also important is remembering that the next quarter will always be thrown about by
special factors, and what matters is where things are in a year or so’s time.

### Page 3

than a new monthly series with its short history and uncertain seasonality that is prone to revision. (And
the RBA might well go back on that commitment, but one must explicitly believe it will.)
The same exercise of leaving pieces out to make a neater story also permeates the dove case. One
could highlight that the 2.1% year-ended GDP growth rate was comprised of two halves, with the most
recent showing much weaker growth in domestic demand. One can point to the larger-than-expected

downturn in the housing market, weak per capita growth, moderate consumption outside a few special
factors such as EV purchases, and the likely run of monthly trimmed mean CPI prints from here being
noticeably below the July figure.
This narrative also requires leaving a few things out, though. As well as the components of the hawk
case already noted, there is the issue that the data centre boom could well offset the receding public
sector’s release of construction resources.

Part of the difficulty is that the narratives we seek can be so black-and-white binary. It is also
embedded in the here and now, immediate data flow. We should ask ourselves whether we should
really take so much signal from small surprises in the latest data. The crucial question is what these
noisy outcomes mean for the underlying trends, and where growth and inflation will be in a year or so.
We also note that much of the recent discussion has made relatively little of the labour market. It has

been easing over much of the past year or so (contra the RBA’s initial assessment earlier this year that
it was re-tightening) and is noticeably softer than RBA expectations. Today’s labour accounts confirm
that more people have taken on secondary jobs to address cost-of-living pressures. Labour supply,
both in terms of the number of people in the labour force and the number of hours they want to work,
has been more resilient than many observers expected. The various measures of underemployment

are pointing to emerging spare capacity that might not be widely recognised.
What is the resolution, if any? Structured analysis including whole-economy models is one way through
the difficulty, but it relies on the models being a decent representation of reality. Perhaps another,
honouring the fact our brains evolved to want simple stories, is to emulate Charles Darwin and
systematically note down any piece of evidence against your current theory. It might make it harder to

tell a simple story, but it will mean you are less likely to miss something that later turns out to be
important.

Document 314: “AI交易”还能继续吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/“AI交易”还能继续吗？.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

科技股全年涨 15% ，但驱动逻辑正在收窄。 Altimeter Capital 创始⼈ Brad Gerstner 警告，
Anthropic 、 OpenAI 和 SpaceX 年化营收年底须从⽬前的 1000 亿增⾄ 1800 亿美元， “AI 交
易 ” 才能⾃洽。前沿实验室的 “ ⽉度营收 ” 已成决定市场⽣死的绝对核⼼。他直⾔： 2026 年，
跟着事实⾛，拒绝杠杠押注。
摘要：
科技股的狂欢仍在持续，但⽀撑这场盛宴的底层逻辑正在经历严苛考验。 Altimeter Capital 创始
⼈兼 CEO Brad Gerstner 在 All-In Podcast 年度峰会上发表演讲指出，当前市场上涨由企业盈利
驱动⽽⾮估值泡沫，但维持 "AI 交易 " 成⽴的关键条件正在收窄 —— 前沿实验室的⽉度营收数据，
将成为决定⾏情能否向上突破的核⼼变量。
Gerstner 表⽰，Anthropic 、 OpenAI 和 SpaceX 三家领先机构当前年化营收合计约 1000 亿美
元，到今年年底这⼀数字⾄少需要增⻓⾄ 1800 亿美元， "AI 交易 " 逻辑⽅能⾃洽。若云巨头持续

推进的资本开⽀扩张最终⽆法被⾜够的租⽤需求承接，当前的激进投资周期将难以为继。
市场层⾯， Gerstner 维持 " 中等仓位 " ，拒绝⾼杠杆冒险。他表⽰，若前沿实验室⽉收⼊能维持在
80 亿美元⾼位，同时油价回落压制⻓端利率，将考虑进⼀步加仓；反之，则保留减仓权利。他
明确警告：2026 年与过去三年截然不同， " 每个⼈都知道 AI ，相关预期已经反映在价格⾥，现在
必须跟着事实⾛，保持灵活。 "
盈利驱动⽽⾮泡沫，但⾏情⾼度集中
科技股年内已上涨 15% ，⾃去年 1 ⽉以来累计涨幅达 39% ，但 Gerstner 强调，这与 2000 年互联⽹
泡沫有本质区别。
当前上涨的核⼼驱动⼒是企业盈利增⻓，⽽⾮估值倍数扩张。今年企业盈利增⻓约 26% ，纳斯
达克与标普 500 的估值倍数反⽽有所收缩。以英伟达为例，其股价当前对应明年完全纳税后
GAAP 利润的市盈率约为 14 倍， " 纳斯达克、标普 500 、费城半导体指数和英伟达的估值，都明显
低于各⾃的历史平均⽔平。 "
然⽽，⾏情的结构性集中令⼈警惕。 Gerstner 指出，半导体板块贡献了纳斯达克指数约 70% 的涨

幅，⽽⾮必需消费品、通⽤软件、⾦融等庞⼤板块⼏乎没有变化。他将这⼀特征称为 " 既是好消
息，也是坏消息 " 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

受益最为直接的是芯⽚与基础设施供应商 —— 即他所称的 "Token 制造者 " 。云巨头的资本开⽀⼏
乎以 1:1 的⽐例转化为基础设施公司的⾃由现⾦流，戴尔股价五年涨 5 倍、 SK Hynix 18 个⽉涨 9
倍，均属⻛险投资级别的回报。
前沿实验室营收爆发，点燃春季⾏情
今年春季科技股的史诗级反弹，直接导⽕索是 Anthropic 的⽉度营收数据。
Gerstner 回顾，Anthropic 的⽉化营收规模在今年 1 ⽉约为 20 亿美元， 2 ⽉升⾄ 40 亿美元， 3 ⽉随
Claude Opus 4.5 与 Claude Code 发布后跃升⾄ 110 亿美元。 " 对于 AI 收⼊会不会出现这个问
题，市场得到了⼀个带着感叹号的答案：收⼊不仅出现了，⽽且规模巨⼤。这就是 4 ⽉和 5 ⽉出
现历史性上涨的原因。 "
进⼊ 6 ⽉、 7 ⽉，市场转为横向整理。 Gerstner 解释， Anthropic 披露的年化营收运⾏率约为 650
亿美元，低于市场此前预期的 750 亿美元，叠加对开源模型追赶的担忧，投资者被迫下调预期。

⽬前， Anthropic 、 OpenAI 和 SpaceX 三家机构的年化营收合计约为 1000 亿美元。 Gerstner 认
为，到今年年底，这⼀数字必须增⻓⾄⾄少 1800 亿⾄ 2000 亿美元，才能⽀撑当前估值逻辑。他
将前沿实验室的⽉度营收数据 —— 究竟是 40 亿美元还是 80 亿美元 —— 定义为 " 当今市场最重要的
数据点 " 。
万亿资本开⽀，谁来买单？
Gerstner 在演讲中援引其与 OpenAI ⾸席执⾏官 Sam Altman 的对话，提出了 AI 投资逻辑的核⼼⽭
盾：⼀家年收⼊仅 130 亿美元的公司，如何承诺消化 1 万亿美元的资本开⽀？
他的框架是：微软、⾕歌、亚⻢逊等云巨头建设数据中⼼是为了对外出租，真正的问题是谁来
租⽤这些算⼒。 " 必须有承购收⼊，才能⽀付租⾦。 "
按照他的测算，即便今年年底 AI 相关年化营收达到 2000 亿美元，仍远低于未来⼏年所需⽔平。
要匹配云巨头持续推进的资本开⽀扩张，这⼀数字须从 2000 亿逐步增⻓⾄ 4500 亿、 8000 亿，乃

作流构成的潜在市场规模，在他看来是 " 世界历史上最⼤的潜在市场 "—— 只需渗透其中约 4% ，
即约 1.2 万亿美元，便⾜以消化当前的全部 AI 资本开⽀。
他同时援引英伟达⾸席执⾏官⻩仁勋两年前的预测 —— 推理需求将增⻓ 10 亿倍 —— 表⽰这⼀判
断正在智能体时代得到兑现：今年全年预计将产⽣ 47 拍（ Quadrillion ）个推理代币，企业客⼾
在 AI 上的⽀出中位数较 18 个⽉前飙升约 17 倍， Codex ⽤⼾数量则在过去 8 个⽉增⻓了 40 倍。
利润率扩张成企业新逻辑
AI 对企业端的渗透，正在改写利润率扩张的速率。
Gerstner 指出， 2015 年⾄ 2025 年间，纳斯达克公司每股收益年均增⻓约 10% ，其中每年来⾃利
润率扩张的贡献约为 38 个基点。他认为， AI 正将这⼀数字推升⾄ 100 个基点以上。
他援引 Uber 和 Snowflake 作为例证： Uber 表⽰公司将维持约 20% 营收增⻓，但不再增加员⼯⼈
数； Snowflake 则在预计约 30% 营收增⻓的同时冻结编制。 " 对这些公司⽽⾔，最⼤的成本项⽬

是员⼯和⼯程师，它们并不是要解雇所有⼈，只是不再按照过去的速度招⼈。 "

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
此外， Gerstner 预计消费级智能体将打开另⼀个万亿美元级市场，并同时带动⼤量推理代币消
耗。
三⼤⻛险：监管、电⼒与利率
Gerstner 将当前 AI 扩张⾯临的核⼼⻛险归纳为三类。
监管层⾯，他认为政策形成过程将持续混乱，但解决⽅案不会落在任何极端。他表⽰赞同 Elon
Musk 提出的同⾏评审机制建议，同时警告不能重蹈过去的覆辙。
电⼒与算⼒建设层⾯，他对市场的激进预期持保留态度。
分析机构 SemiAnalysis 的 Dylan Patel 预测，明年全球将新增 43 吉⽡算⼒，与美国⽬前不⾜ 40 吉
⽡的算⼒总存量相当。 Gerstner 认为这⼀预测过于激进，受制于审批许可、电⽹互连排队、⾼精
设备断货及技术⼯⼈短缺，实际落地规模可能仅在 25 吉⽡左右，其中约⼀半将流向 Anthropic 与

OpenAI 。
他同时表⽰， 25 吉⽡已⾜够⽀撑前沿实验室实现明年的营收⽬标 —— 据报道 Anthropic 以约 1.5 吉
⽡算⼒实现了今年约 100 亿⾄ 110 亿美元的营收，若再增加 4 ⾄ 5 吉⽡，理论上可⽀持额外 1000 亿
美元的营收增⻓。
利率层⾯， Gerstner 表⽰，明天美联储加息概率已超过 90% ，借贷成本上升将直接推⾼数据中⼼
融资⻔槛。他援引巴菲特的说法： " 利率之于资产价格，就像重⼒之于物质。 " 若 10 年期美债收益
率升⾄ 5.5% ，在⽆需承担股票⻛险即可获得 5.5% ⾄ 6% 回报的情况下，股市估值将⾯临显著压
⼒。
2026 年：跟着事实⾛，拒绝⾼杠杆
Gerstner 以 " ⻜⾏路径 " ⽐喻其投资框架，将当前仓位定为 " 中等 " ，并明确拒绝⾼杠杆策略。
他描述了两个情景：若前沿实验室⽉度营收维持在 80 亿美元⾼位，叠加标志性 AI 公司完成 IPO ，
⾏情有望进⼀步向上突破；若 10 年期美债收益率受油价拖累冲⾄ 5.5% ，或监管介⼊⼲扰前沿实

验室上市节奏，市场将⾯临回踩压⼒。
他特别点名警告使⽤ 4 倍杠杆式投机策略的⻛险，在⾼波动、⾼利率环境下， " 在当前市场⾥使⽤
杠杆⾮常危险 " 。
对于过去三年与未来的投资逻辑转变， Gerstner 的判断简洁⽽直接：2023 年⾄ 2025 年，投资者
只需要押注⼀件事 ——AI 将成为科技史上最⼤超级周期，然后重仓等待收益。 " 但 2026 年已经不
是那种环境，每个⼈都知道 AI ，相关预期也已经反映在价格⾥。现在必须根据事实和具体情况
作出判断，保持思维灵活，跟随事实。 "
